Abstract: We present a reinforcement learning-optimized Rydberg electrometer based on the asymmetric blockade effect and achieve high-sensitivity electric field sensing in Rydberg arrays. Microwave dressing induces asymmetric blockade to suppress interactions between target atoms, while keeping the coupling between the central control atom and target atoms field-tunable near Förster resonance. The field-regulated blockade radius affects the detectable atomic population signals, thereby enabling electric field sensing via state-selective readout. In planar atomic arrays, classical Fisher information exhibits near-quadratic scaling with atom number and approaches the Heisenberg limit. Reinforcement learning-designed composite pulses greatly enhance quantum Fisher information by up to one order of magnitude compared with single $π$ pulses. We further establish a compact six-atom spherical configuration for vector electrometry, in which field orientation is extracted from calibrated axial populations, and weak bias fields eliminate dipole-dipole-induced sign and magic-angle ambiguities. Numerical tests against Rabi frequency deviation, positional error, residual inter-target coupling and projection noise demonstrate the reliability of this scheme. This work provides an experimentally viable approach to realize high-precision three-dimensional Rydberg electric field sensing.

</details>


### [99] [The Pangaea Architecture: Fault-Tolerant Heterogeneous Topological Codes via a Quantum Bus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01887)
*Sheir Yarkoni,Chen Scheim,Daniel Hakshuri,Nadav Katz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Pangaea，一种利用量子总线（quantum bus）在远程拓扑码补丁之间协调逻辑操作的容错量子架构。该总线是一种辅助规范码条，其测量能够重构联合逻辑算符，同时保持最近邻物理连接。量子总线可被视为晶格手术的三维推广，支持异构拓扑码和多量子比特泡利操作。相比传统二维架构，Pangaea仅需O(dN_L)个物理量子比特即可实现N_L个距离为d的逻辑量子比特的多量子比特交互（传统为O(d²N_L)）。在50逻辑量子比特尺度上，匹配逻辑错误率下Pangaea比平面表面码架构最多少用10倍物理量子比特。作者通过伪阈值模拟验证了长程测量辅助CNOT原语（表面-表面和表面-颜色联合宇称测量）的容错性，并利用此协议构建了基于量子总线的原生异构15对1魔法态蒸馏模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平面晶格手术架构在二维布局中面临路由瓶颈，逻辑量子比特之间的远距离交互需要大量辅助物理量子比特（O(d²N_L)），导致物理资源开销过高，限制了容错量子计算的扩展能力。作者旨在提出一种三维推广的架构来解决这一路由瓶颈，同时保持容错性和最近邻物理连接约束。

Method: Pangaea架构的核心创新是量子总线：一条辅助规范码条（gauge-code strip），其测量可以重构远程补丁之间的联合逻辑算符，无需额外物理路由。该架构支持异构拓扑码（如表面码与颜色码混合）以及多量子比特泡利操作，本质上将晶格手术扩展到三维。作者通过伪阈值模拟验证长程测量辅助CNOT原语的容错性，并利用量子总线构建原生异构15对1魔法态蒸馏模块。

Result: Pangaea仅需O(dN_L)物理量子比特实现多逻辑量子比特交互，相比传统二维架构的O(d²N_L)显著降低资源开销。在50逻辑量子比特尺度、匹配逻辑错误率条件下，比平面表面码架构最多减少10倍物理量子比特用量。表面-表面和表面-颜色联合宇称测量的长程CNOT原语通过伪阈值模拟验证了容错性。

Conclusion: Pangaea确立了一种可扩展的三维容错量子计算架构，通过量子总线有效解决了平面晶格手术的路由瓶颈，在物理资源效率上具有显著优势，为大规模容错量子计算提供了一条可行的实现路径。

Abstract: We introduce Pangaea, a fault-tolerant quantum architecture that uses a quantum bus to mediate logical operations between remote patches of two-dimensional topological codes. The bus is an auxiliary gauge-code strip whose measurements reconstruct joint logical operators while preserving nearest-neighbor physical connectivity. Enabling native heterogeneous topological codes and multi-qubit Pauli operations, the quantum bus can be interpreted as a three-dimensional generalization of lattice surgery. We require only $O(dN_L)$ physical qubits to implement multi-qubit interactions for $N_L$ distance-$d$ logical qubits, compared to $O(d^2N_L)$ of traditional two-dimensional architectures. At the 50-logical-qubit scale, Pangaea uses up to $10\times$ fewer physical qubits than planar surface-code architectures at matched logical error rates. We verify fault-tolerance of long-range measurement-based CNOT primitives for both surface--surface and surface--color joint parity measurements using pseudo-threshold simulations. We use this protocol to construct a native heterogeneous 15-to-1 magic-state distillation module using the quantum bus. These results establish Pangaea as a scalable architecture for three-dimensional fault-tolerant quantum computing that resolves the routing bottleneck of planar lattice surgery.

</details>


### [100] [Oraqle: An Empirical Analysis of Qubit Readout and Discriminators in Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01939)
*Emmanouil Giortamis,Aleksandra Świerkowska,Sandra Stankovic,Felix Gust,Benjamin Lienhard,Pramod Bhatotia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Oraqle，一个端到端基准测试框架，用于评估量子比特态读出及其对量子纠错（QEC）性能的影响。该框架基于真实实验提取的读出数据集、最先进的机器学习鉴别器、多种QEC码和不同硬件规模进行评估，并揭示了三个不对称发现：测量时长可大幅缩短而几乎不损失逻辑错误率；鉴别器复杂度对QEC性能影响甚微；读出对逻辑错误率的影响取决于硬件在QEC发展中的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错是迈向容错量子计算的最有希望的路径，而读出在QEC循环中扮演关键角色——它是解码器获得真相的唯一来源，同时也是堆栈中最慢、最容易出错的操作。然而，QEC性能尚未从读出特性、读出长度及其与机器学习鉴别器协同设计的角度进行深入研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一个系统性的评估框架。

Method: Oraqle框架采用端到端的方式，整合了四个关键组件：1）真实实验提取的量子比特态读出数据集；2）最先进的机器学习鉴别器模型；3）多种QEC码；4）从当前设备到未来设备的多种硬件范式。通过这一综合框架，系统地评估读出特性对QEC性能的影响。

Result: 研究发现了三个不对称的结果：第一，测量时长可显著缩短而几乎不增加逻辑错误率；第二，鉴别器的模型复杂度对QEC性能影响极小，因为残余错误被写入设备物理特性而非模型本身；第三，量子比特态读出对逻辑错误率的影响取决于硬件在QEC发展格局中的位置，且随着设备成熟这一影响窗口会扩大。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了读出特性与QEC性能之间复杂且非直觉的关系，为量子读出系统的优化方向提供了重要指导，表明在QEC设计中对读出时长、鉴别器复杂度等参数进行协同优化具有显著价值。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) is the most promising route toward fault-tolerant quantum computing and, thus, useful quantum computers. QEC operates as a continuous measure-decode-correct cycle: ancilla qubits are read out, a decoder infers errors from the resulting syndromes, and corrections are applied before the next round begins. Within this loop, readout occupies a uniquely critical role, as it is the sole source of ground truth available to the decoder. Yet readout is also the slowest and most error-prone operation in the stack, with characteristics that vary across qubits and drift over time; This complexity propagates directly to the classical control hardware, and in particular to the FPGA-hosted machine-learning (ML) discriminator that must classify each analog signal into a binary syndrome outcome. Despite this central role, QEC performance has not yet been studied in depth from the perspective of readout characteristics, readout length, and their co-design with an ML discriminator.
  We introduce Oraqle, an end-to-end benchmarking framework that evaluates qubit-state readout and its impact on QEC performance across real experimentally extracted qubit-state-readout datasets, state-of-the-art ML discriminators, multiple QEC codes, and hardware regimes spanning current to projected devices. Our study reveals three asymmetric findings: The measurement duration can be significantly reduced with nearly no penalty to the logical error rate; The discriminator complexity barely affects the QEC performance, as residual errors are written into device physics rather than the model; and the impact of qubit-state readout on the logical error rate is conditional on where the hardware sits in the QEC landscape, a window that widens as devices mature.

</details>


### [101] [Zero-G: A Pre-Decoder-Aware Decoder for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02030)
*Peter Wegmann,Theofilos Augoustis,Aleksandra Świerkowska,Emmanouil Giortamis,Pramod Bhatotia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Zero-G是一种为配合预解码器设计的强解码器，通过随机近似最小权重完美匹配（MWPM）算法利用稀疏残差综合征，在匹配精度下实现比现有强解码器快10倍的延迟，最坏情况亚350ns解码，并支持跨FPGA和CPU的异构部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要解码器跟上硬件速度以避免指数级延迟积压。虽然预解码器理论上应加速强解码器，但现有强解码器是为稠密综合征设计的，无法利用预解码器提供的稀疏性，导致实际加速效果甚微。

Method: 提出Zero-G强解码器，基于随机近似MWPM算法，动态利用稀疏残差综合征，以延迟-精度权衡替代全有或全无的运行时间-精度权衡；同时将硬件控制与解码核心解耦，实现FPGA和CPU上的异构部署。

Result: Zero-G在匹配精度下相比现有强解码器实现10倍延迟改进，最坏情况亚350ns解码（码距最高d=15），在单个128核CPU上可扩展至640逻辑量子比特，单个AMD Versal V80 FPGA上可扩展至32逻辑量子比特。

Conclusion: Zero-G通过与预解码器协同设计，有效解决了现有强解码器无法利用稀疏综合征的问题，在保持精度的同时显著提升了解码速度，并实现了跨硬件平台的灵活部署。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires classical decoders that keep pace with the underlying hardware, translating syndrome measurements into corrections fast enough to avoid an exponential backlog. To meet this real-time constraint, pre-decoders have emerged as part of a hierarchical decoding approach to resolve simple, local errors before passing a sparser residual syndrome to a strong decoder. While pre-decoding should, in theory, speed up the strong decoder, in practice, the speedup is only marginal, since existing strong decoders are designed to decode dense syndromes and cannot exploit the sparsity provided by pre-decoders. To address this, we present Zero-G, a strong decoder designed for use alongside pre-decoders. As a stochastic approximate minimum-weight perfect matching (MWPM) decoder, Zero-G exploits sparse residual syndromes, dynamically trading latency for accuracy rather than relying on an all-or-nothing runtime-accuracy trade-off. By decoupling hardware control from the decoding core itself, we enable heterogeneous deployment across both FPGAs and CPUs without maintaining separate implementations. Zero-G achieves a $10\times$ latency improvement over existing strong decoders at matching accuracy, with worst-case sub-350ns decoding at code distances up to d=15, while scaling to 640 logical qubits on a single 128-core CPU and 32 logical qubits on a single AMD Versal V80 FPGA.

</details>


### [102] [Convergence monitoring of quantum Gibbs samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02038)
*Nikolaos Louloudis,Ruben Ibarrondo,Mikel Sanz,Mario Berta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于量子吉布斯采样器的低成本收敛判据，利用弱测量记录中的能量交换平衡条件来诊断采样是否达到热平衡，无需任何系统哈密顿量信息，仅基于采样器已生成的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全量子马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法虽已实现高效吉布斯态采样，但严格的混合时间最坏情况界在经典难解系统中仍然难以获得。实际经验表明相关可观测量可能快速收敛，因此需要一种高效（多项式开销内）的收敛诊断工具。

Method: 利用量子吉布斯采样器及其量子比特高效变体中固有的弱测量记录，观察到在热平衡状态下系统与环境之间的净能量流为零，能量交换统计满足平衡条件；该条件体现在弱测量记录中提取的（准）频率分布中，据此构建一个与哈密顿量无关的停止判据，仅依赖采样器已产生的数据。

Result: 提供了所提判据的统计分析，并通过数值研究和解析研究考察了其性能、假设条件和局限性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种实用且低成本的量子吉布斯采样收敛监测方案，使混合时间无法从理论上保证的场景下仍能有效判断采样是否达到热平衡，且无需额外的计算开销或对系统哈密顿量的先验知识。

Abstract: Recent progress in fully quantum Markov chain Monte Carlo methods enables efficient Gibbs-state sampling on quantum computers [Chen et al., Nature 646, 561 (2025)]. Although rigorous worst-case bounds on mixing times remain largely inaccessible for classically intractable systems, experience from classical Monte Carlo suggests that convergence of relevant observables may nevertheless be rapid. This raises the practical question of how to diagnose convergence efficiently, i.e., with at most polynomial overhead. We propose a low-cost criterion for convergence monitoring that exploits the weak measurements inherent in quantum Gibbs samplers and their qubit-efficient variants [Ding et al., arXiv:2508.05703 (2025)]. Our approach is based on the observation that, at thermal equilibrium, the net energy flow between system and environment vanishes and energy-exchange statistics satisfy a balance condition. This condition appears in the distribution of (quasi-)frequencies extracted from the weak-measurement record and we use it to construct a Hamiltonian-agnostic stopping criterion based solely on data already generated by the sampler. We provide a statistical analysis, along with numerical and analytical studies to understand its performance, assumptions, and limitations.

</details>


### [103] [Towards Tensor-Network SAT-Solvers for Quantum-Classical Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02041)
*Benjamin Zec,Lukas Schmidbauer,Maja Franz,Wolfgang Mauerer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了张量网络基态搜索作为经典替代品（surrogate）用于高性能计算与量子计算（HPC/QC）集成系统中的优化问题。通过对比原生高阶Ising编码与二次化QUBO编码在Max-3-SAT上的DMRG求解性能，发现二次化并非中性变换，会严重降低解质量，且模拟退火在所有测试实例上匹配或优于DMRG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成HPC/QC系统不能仅作为量子内核分发机制，需要支持可观测性、回退执行以及基于成本的决策（是否用量子任务的经典替代品替换）。这些替代机制必须近似或利用问题结构来缓解不可避免的指数级经典最坏情况复杂度。因此需要研究合适的经典替代品及其选择标准。

Method: 以张量网络基态搜索（结合矩阵乘积算符MPO编码和密度矩阵重整化群DMRG方法）作为经典替代品，对Max-3-SAT进行两种编码对比：原生多项式无约束优化到高阶Ising（higher-order Ising）和二次化QUBO到二次Ising。以模拟退火（SA）作为经典性能基线，比较各方案在解质量和端到端工具链效果上的表现。

Result: 结果显示：二次化并非中性变换步骤，辅助变量和成对耦合显著降低了相对于原生高阶表示的解质量；模拟退火在所有测试实例上匹配或优于DMRG。由于SAT派生问题的最优解是经典乘积态，DMRG在纠缠方面的优势在此类问题上无法体现。

Conclusion: HPC/QC运行时中的替代品选择必须对编码和实例有感知（encoding- and instance-aware）；这些经验证据为回退策略和架构协同设计中的知情决策提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Integrated HPC/QC systems aim to combine classical high-performance computing with quantum processors, but cannot be reduced to mechanisms for dispatching quantum kernels. An integrated architecture must support aspects such as observability, which cannot be implemented using QPUs alone, as well as fallback execution and cost-aware decisions on whether to replace quantum tasks with classical surrogates. Such mechanisms must be approximate or benefit from problem structure to soften the inescapable exponential classical worst-case complexity. In this work, we study tensor-network ground-state search, as such a surrogate, for optimisation problems. This combines key quantum primitives with advanced classical simulation. It provides initial empirical indicators for surrogate selection criteria, and exposes end-to-end toolchain effects that may be missed when transformation steps are studied in isolation. We compare a native polynomial unconstrained optimisation to-higher-order-Ising and a quadratised quadratic unconstrained binary optimization to-quadratic-Ising formulation for Max-3-SAT. Both are encoded as matrix product operator and optimised using density matrix renormalisation group approaches, with simulated annealing (SA) as classical performance baseline. Our results show that quadratisation is not a neutral transformation step: auxiliary variables and pairwise couplings substantially degrade solution quality relative to the native higher-order representation, while SA matches or outperforms DMRG across all tested instances. Since the optima of Boolean satisfiability (SAT)-derived problems are classical product states, DMRGs advantages dont materialise here. These findings suggest that surrogate selection in HPC/QC runtimes must be encoding- and instance-aware and provide empirical groundwork for informed decisions on fallback strategies and architecture co-design.

</details>


### [104] [Adaptive Reconstruction of Bosonic Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02049)
*Vasilisa Usova,Phila Rembold,Ian Yang,Marco Rossignolo,Simone Montangero,Samuele Tosatto,Gerhard Kirchmair*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应重构技术，用于估计玻色子量子态相对一族态的保真度，同时从少量测量中重构Wigner函数，并在电路量子电动力学平台上对薛定谔猫态进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色子量子系统是硬件高效的量子信息处理平台，但因其巨大的希尔伯特空间和高测量成本而难以表征。现有方法仅能估计相对单一目标态的保真度，无法处理物理等效但存在相空间平移、旋转等变换的态族。需要一种能够对一族玻色子态进行保真度估计且测量高效的方法。

Method: 该方法将物理信息参数化模型与贝叶斯推断、自助法和主动学习相结合，迭代选择最具信息量的相空间采样点。通过自适应采样以少量测量重构Wigner函数并估计相对一族玻色子态的保真度。

Result: 在电路量子电动力学平台上，对振幅α∈[1,3]的薛定谔猫态进行了基准测试。重构在几分钟内产生可重现的保真度估计，对相空间中的大幅位移和旋转保持鲁棒（即使使用不匹配的先验），并能敏感地检测细微的态缺陷。将自适应策略与现有Wigner函数采样协议进行了实验比较，证明了自适应采样相对于一族猫态保真度估计的优势。最后，将重构的保真度纳入闭环量子最优控制实验的度量标准中，验证了该方法在自主优化玻色子量子态方面的适用性。

Conclusion: 该自适应重构方法为玻色子量子态的表征提供了一种测量高效且对变换稳健的方案，可推广到更广泛的态族保真度估计，并成功集成到自主优化的闭环控制框架中，展示了实际应用的潜力。

Abstract: Bosonic quantum systems provide a hardware-efficient platform for quantum information processing but remain challenging to characterise due to their large Hilbert space and the high measurement cost of state tomography. Existing approaches estimate the fidelity with respect to a single target state, making them unsuitable for applications in which physically equivalent states differ by phase space translations, rotations, or other transformations. Here, we introduce an adaptive reconstruction technique that estimates the fidelity with respect to a family of bosonic states while reconstructing the underlying Wigner function from a small number of measurements. The method combines a physics-informed parametric model with Bayesian inference, bootstrap, and active learning to iteratively select the most informative phase space sampling points. We implement the approach on a circuit quantum electrodynamics platform and benchmark it on Schrödinger cat states with amplitudes $α\in[1,3]$. The reconstruction yields reproducible fidelity estimates within a few minutes, remains robust to substantial displacements and rotations in phase space despite using a mismatched prior, and is sensitive to subtle state imperfections. We further compare the adaptive strategy with existing Wigner function sampling protocols experimentally, demonstrating the advantage of adaptive sampling for measurement-efficient fidelity estimation with respect to a family of cat states. Finally, we incorporate the reconstructed fidelity into the figure of merit used in a proof-of-principle closed-loop quantum optimal control experiment, demonstrating the applicability of the method to autonomous optimisation of bosonic quantum states.

</details>


### [105] [Scaled Caldeira-Leggett Dynamics: Deterministic Trajectories and the Classical Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02054)
*S. V. Mousavi,S. Miret-Artés*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Bohmian框架下研究了Caldeira-Leggett模型中开放量子系统在高温区域的动力学。通过将密度矩阵写为极坐标形式，获得了广义连续性和Hamilton-Jacobi方程。研究发现有效势在经典极限下不消失且不能简单分解为量子和热贡献之和，导致温度依赖的经典轨迹在热环境中仍然保持确定性，这与Langevin描述中的随机动力学形成对比。通过引入量子性参数构建了缩放的Caldeira-Leggett方程，应用于高斯态展示了相干性和干涉的渐进抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开放量子系统在高温热环境中的量子到经典的转变机制，特别是探究热效应对有效势的影响及其在不同描述框架（Bohmian vs Langevin）下的差异。

Method: 采用Bohmian（波导）语言分析Caldeira-Leggett模型的高温动力学：将密度矩阵表示为极坐标形式，推导广义连续性方程和广义Hamilton-Jacobi方程；引入量子性参数对密度矩阵和Planck常数进行缩放，构建缩放的Caldeira-Leggett方程，并通过高斯态进行验证。

Result: 推导出包含显式热效应的广义连续性方程以及通过有效势间接受热影响的Hamilton-Jacobi方程。有效势在经典极限下不消失且不可分解为可加的量子和热贡献，保留非平凡结构。温度相关的经典轨迹保持确定性而非随机性。缩放方程对高斯态的应用揭示了由热效应和转变参数共同控制的相干性及干涉的渐进抑制。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了Bohmian框架下开放量子系统动力学中有效势的非平凡结构，表明在热环境中量子到经典的转变由热效应和量子性参数共同主导，且Bohmian描述（确定性）与Langevin描述（随机性）本质上体现了不同视角下的物理图景。

Abstract: The dynamics of an open quantum system in the high-temperature regime of the Caldeira-Leggett model using a Bohmian language is investigated. By expressing the density matrix in polar form, generalized continuity and Hamilton-Jacobi equations are obtained. Thermal effects appear explicitly in the former, while they influence the latter only indirectly through an effective potential. Remarkably, the effective potential does not vanish in the classical limit and cannot, in general, be decomposed into simple additive quantum and thermal contributions. Instead, it retains a nontrivial structure leading to a residual temperature dependence in the resulting dynamics. As a consequence, the resulting temperature-dependent-classical trajectories remain deterministic even in the presence of a thermal environment. This behavior contrasts with the stochastic dynamics observed in the Langevin description and reflects the ensemble-based nature of the present approach. To further explore the quantum-to-classical transition, a scaled version of the Caldeira-Leggett equation is introduced by scaling both the density matrix and Planck constant through a quantumness parameter. This parameter takes the value one in the fully quantum regime and smoothly approaches zero in the classical limit. Applications to Gaussian states demonstrate a gradual suppression of coherence and interference, governed jointly by thermal effects and the transition parameter.

</details>


### [106] [Characterizing the nitrogen-vacancy center singlet transition and its phonon sideband for absorption-based room-temperature magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02060)
*Tobias Probst,Florian Schall,Sebastian Heuft,Janina J. Schindler,Lukas Lindner,Sven Mägdefessel,Rüdiger Quay,Philipp Reineck,Alexander M. Zaitsev,Takeshi Ohshima,Matthias Weidemüller,Jan Jeske*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用腔增强光谱学方法研究金刚石中氮空位（NV）中心的吸收型磁力测定技术。在室温680-1050 nm光谱范围内表征了泵浦激光和微波诱导的腔信号变化，通过归一化消除腔增强效应，获得了准单程吸收和ODMR对比度数据，并利用一种新型微波诱导信号变化方法测定了单态吸收截面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于NV中心的磁力测定在近年来显示出巨大潜力。传统的基于NV⁻三重态荧光读出的技术存在对比度和灵敏度限制，而基于吸收的磁力测定技术通过腔增强吸收长度可以改善对比度和灵敏度。作者希望通过腔增强光谱学方法系统研究吸收型磁力测定技术，以获得更好的磁力测定性能。

Method: 采用腔增强光谱学方法：1）在室温680-1050 nm光谱范围内研究泵浦激光和微波诱导的腔信号变化；2）通过归一化消除腔增强效应，提供准单程吸收值和ODMR对比度；3）利用一种新方法通过微波诱导信号变化测量宽带单态吸收截面，该方法对泵浦激光引起的其他缺陷信号变化不敏感。

Result: 1）最高对比度出现在1042 nm处，但声子边带的多处峰值也呈现高对比度；2）腔增强ODMR对比度达到50-80%；3）1042 nm处吸收截面测定为σ=(0.89±0.14)×10⁻²¹ m²或(2.9±0.5)×10⁻²¹ m²，取决于所采用的532 nm NV⁻吸收截面值。

Conclusion: 腔增强吸收型磁力测定技术能够提供较高的ODMR对比度（50-80%），改进传统荧光读出方法的性能。同时，论文提供了一种新颖且可靠的室温单态吸收截面测量方法，为NV中心磁力测定的定量化研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Magnetometry with nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond has shown great promise in recent years. In particular, absorption-based magnetometry techniques, employing a cavity to enhance the absorption length, can improve the contrast and sensitivity compared to conventional techniques based on reading out the NV$^-$ triplet fluorescence. The absorption techniques rely on magnetic-field-dependent absorption at the NV$^-$ singlet zero phonon line at 1042$\,$nm and its phonon sideband. In a cavity-enhanced spectroscopy approach, we study pump-laser- and microwave-induced cavity signal changes at room temperature over a spectral range of 680-1050$\,$nm. Through normalization, we eliminate the cavity-enhancement effect and provide quasi-single-pass values for the absorption and optically detected magnetic resonance (ODMR) contrast. The highest contrast is found at 1042$\,$nm, but multiple points of high contrast are found at the peaks of the phonon sideband. Additionally, cavity-enhanced ODMR contrasts in the range of 50-80$\,\%$ are presented. We further measure the broadband singlet absorption cross section at room temperature with a novel method through microwave-induced signal changes. This method is insensitive to pump-laser-induced signal changes by other defects and quantifies the room-temperature absorption strength of the singlet transition and its entire phonon sideband. We determine the absorption cross section at 1042$\,$nm to be $σ^{\,\bigstar}_{1042}=(0.89\pm0.14)\cdot 10^{-21}\,\text{m}^2$ or $σ^{\,\blacktriangle}_{1042}=(2.9\pm0.5)\cdot 10^{-21}\,\text{m}^2$. depending on the employed 532$\,$nm NV$^-$ absorption cross section.

</details>


### [107] [Efficient re-sampling in quasi-probability decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02075)
*Sara Santos,Stefan Woerner,Vincenzo Savona,Julien Gacon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对拟概率分解（QPD）的重加权策略，通过在不同参数设置下重用样本，降低了具有相同变分结构的电路中的采样开销，并将该策略应用于参数化量子态保真度估计和量子几何张量实部估计，在现实硬件噪声下优于其他标准估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期量子设备受噪声和硬件限制，需要算法层面的方法用增加采样开销换取电路复杂度降低。QPD可以将非局域操作替换为多个局域操作电路，但其采样开销通常呈指数增长，严重限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出一种针对QPD的重加权策略：对于在不同参数设置下具有相同变分结构的电路，通过重用样本（reweighting samples）来降低采样开销。首先将该方法用于参数化量子态之间的保真度估计（变分时间演化和量子核方法的关键原语），然后结合同步扰动随机逼近（SPSA）估计量子几何张量的实部。

Result: 该方法能够控制QPD指数级采样开销，同时保留态编码拟设的结构。在存在现实硬件噪声的情况下，该方法在估计量子几何张量实部时优于其他标准估计技术。

Conclusion: 重加权策略有潜力扩展基于QPD的方法在变分量子算法中的适用性，为缓解QPD采样开销指数增长问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Near-term quantum devices are limited by noise and hardware constraints, motivating algorithmic approaches that trade circuit complexity for increased sampling overhead. Quasi-probability decompositions (QPDs), for example, allow replacing non-local operations by multiple circuits with local operations, but the associated sampling overhead generally scales exponentially and limits their practicality. In this work, we introduce a reweighting strategy for QPDs for circuits with the same variational structure across parameter settings, reusing samples and thereby reducing the sampling overhead. We first demonstrate this approach by estimating fidelities between parameterized quantum states, a key primitive in variational time evolution and quantum kernel methods. Importantly, this setup allows controlling the exponential QPD sampling overhead while preserving the structure of the state-encoding ansatz. We then apply the method to estimate the real part of the quantum geometric tensor using the simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation and find that, in the presence of realistic hardware noise, our method outperforms other standard estimation techniques. These results highlight the potential of reweighting strategies to extend the applicability of QPD-based methods in variational quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [108] [Field-Free Transverse Aharonov--Bohm Phase Gate for an Orbital $l$-Qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02090)
*Ju Gao,Fang Shen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了受限在有限壁环形波导中的自旋分辨迪拉克电流，发现有限壁约束将电子固有的方位角Aharonov-Bohm（AB）流转化为直行传播模式的横向AB（TAB）传播相位。通过保留完整的倏逝尾场将径向梯度流闭合为边界贡献，最终得到自旋无关的轨道相位Δφ∝lΦL⟨ρ⁻²⟩/v_z。利用|±l⟩反绕模式构建同路径量子比特实现R_z(2δ_l)旋转，在100μm波导、a=20nm、R=30nm、E_z=10meV、|l|=10参数下灵敏度为0.1885 mrad/mG，R_z(π)对应16.67 G磁场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间中的AB贝塞尔模式已证明携带有磁通依赖的方位角薛定谔概率流和动力学轨道角动量。本文的动机是将这一响应推广到自旋分辨的守恒迪拉克电流场景（适用于类石墨烯/拓扑系统），探索有限壁约束下环形波导中AB效应的新表现形式，并期望构建一种无磁场（field-free）的导引相位操作器件。

Method: 采用核心排除的有限壁环形波导模型，分析自旋分辨的守恒迪拉克电流。通过分离仍存活的轨道成分并将其转化为直行传播模式的横向AB（TAB）传播相位；利用完整的倏逝尾场将径向梯度流闭合为边界贡献；研究自旋反转下径向梯度流的方向反转及最终的相位自旋无关性；分析|±l⟩反绕模式作为同路径量子比特的R_z(2δ_l)实现及串扰条件（需要角谐波m=±2l）。

Result: 有限壁约束将固有方位角迪拉克电流转化为无磁场导引相位操作。径向梯度流在自旋反转下反向，保留完整倏逝尾场后其作为边界贡献闭合，留下自旋无关的轨道相位Δφ∝lΦL⟨ρ⁻²⟩_ln/v_z。|±l⟩模式构成同路径量子比特可实现R_z(2δ_l)，含微分读出与共模相位抑制，直接一阶串扰要求角谐波m=±2l。在a=20nm、R=30nm、E_z=10meV、|l|=10的100μm波导中，灵敏度为0.1885 mrad/mG，R_z(π)在16.67 G处实现。

Conclusion: 本文证明有限壁环形波导约束可以将内在的方位角迪拉克电流转变为一个无磁场的导引相位操作。该工作为基于AB效应的量子相位调控提供了新的器件架构，实现了自旋无关、可差分读出且具有共模抑制能力的量子比特旋转操作。

Abstract: Free-space Aharonov--Bohm (AB) Bessel modes are known to carry a flux-dependent azimuthal Schrödinger probability current and kinetic orbital angular momentum. We extend this response to the spin-resolved conserved Dirac current of a core-excluded finite-wall annular guide and show that confinement converts its surviving orbital component into a transverse Aharonov--Bohm (TAB) propagation phase for a straight traveling mode whose centroid path has zero projection onto $\mathbf A$. The radial-gradient current reverses under spin reversal; retaining the complete evanescent tail closes it as a boundary contribution, leaving a spin-independent orbital phase $Δφ_{ln}\propto lΦL_{\rm int}\langleρ^{-2}\rangle_{ln}/v_z$. Opposite-winding modes $|\pm l\rangle$ form a same-path qubit implementing $R_z(2δ_l)$ with differential readout and common-mode phase rejection, while direct first-order crosstalk requires the angular harmonic $m=\pm2l$. For a $100\,μ\mathrm{m}$ section with $a=20\,\mathrm{nm}$, $R=30\,\mathrm{nm}$, $E_z=10\,\mathrm{meV}$, and $|l|=10$, the sensitivity is $0.1885\,\mathrm{mrad/mG}$ and $R_z(π)$ occurs at $16.67\,\mathrm{G}$. Finite-wall confinement thus turns intrinsic azimuthal Dirac current into a guided field-free phase operation.

</details>


### [109] [Better accuracy with fewer qubits: Single-particle basis set optimization for quantum chemistry on quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02119)
*Subimal Deb,V. S. Prasannaa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子计算友好的改良最小基组（MSTO-kG），通过遗传算法优化并结合激进细化策略生成H~F原子的基组。该基组在FCI水平下计算的原子基态能量与6-31G相当或更优（Li甚至超越cc-pVQZ），在保持相同或更少量子比特的情况下实现更高精度的量子化学计算。对于分子体系（除H2外），MSTO基组的FCI/CISD结果与6-31G相当或更优，且VQE、QPE和HHL所需的量子比特、双量子比特门和T门数量均显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的量子计算机存在显著噪声，限制了量子化学计算可处理的轨道数量。然而，即使使用质量合理的单粒子基组，小活性空间（少量轨道）也会损失大量相关能。因此，需要设计适合量子算法的高能效（qubit-efficient）基组，在有限量子资源下尽可能保留更多电子相关能。

Method: 采用遗传算法（genetic algorithm-inspired）结合激进细化策略（aggressive refinement strategies），重新优化现有最小基组，生成H到F原子的改良最小基组MSTO-kG（k=2-11）。在FCI、CISD水平下评估原子和分子的基态能量，并与6-31G、cc-pVQZ等标准基组对比；同时通过VQE、QPE和HHL算法比较资源消耗（量子比特数、双量子比特门数、T门数）。

Result: MSTO基组在FCI水平下，H到F的原子基态能量与6-31G相当或更低，Li甚至超越cc-pVQZ。分子方面，H2表现不佳（与文献一致），但Li2、C2、LiH、BeH和BeH2的FCI/CISD结果与6-31G相当或更优。资源对比表明，MSTO基组在VQE、QPE和HHL算法中消耗更少的量子比特和双量子比特门，QPE和HHL的逻辑T门数也显著降低。

Conclusion: 本文工作为近端（near-term）量子计算机上的量子化学计算开辟了新途径：通过定制化的能效基组，在减少量子资源消耗的同时提升或保持计算精度，使更准确但更少量子比特需求的量子化学计算成为可能。

Abstract: In spite of recent advances, quantum computers are expected to be sufficiently noisy in the coming few years to the extent of limiting quantum chemical calculations to relatively small number of orbitals. However, even with reasonable quality single particle basis sets, small active spaces with limited orbitals can result in a significant fraction of correlation energy being lost, motivating the design of moderate quality qubit-efficient basis sets for quantum algorithms. We begin by reoptimizing the existing minimal basis sets using a genetic algorithm-inspired approach in conjunction with aggressive refinement strategies, and generate modified minimal basis sets (MSTO-kG basis; k = 2-11) for atoms from H through F. The ground state energies of H through F using our MSTO bases at FCI level of theory yield ground state energies that are comparable or sometimes even lower than those obtained using 6-31G basis sets. In the case of Li, the MSTO bases surpass the performance of cc-pVQZ bases. Thus, we obtain better atomic energies with same number of qubits relative to STO bases, and better/comparable energies with fewer qubits relative to higher quality bases. In the case of molecules, H2 performs poorly; a finding that is consistent with an earlier work in literature. For other molecules, Li2, C2, LiH, BeH and BeH2, the FCI results (except C2 for which we employ CISD) from our bases are comparable to/outperform those from 6-31G basis. Finally, we compare the resources required between different bases and find that MSTO bases yield better energies than the competing basis sets while incurring fewer qubits and two-qubit gates with VQE, QPE, and HHL. The logical T-gate counts are also found to be considerably lower for QPE and HHL respectively. Overall, our work paves way for more accurate yet less qubit-hungry quantum chemical calculations using near-term quantum computers.

</details>


### [110] [Unconditionally successful quantum Time-Marching algorithm via LCU for nonlinear Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02130)
*Niccolo Fonio,Giuseppe Di Molfetta,Pierre Sagaut*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子格子气体算法，实现非线偏微分方程（如Burgers方程）的无条件成功量子模拟，首次实现时间步可无限串联而不出现概率性失败的量子算法。核心思想是利用线性组合酉算子框架中量子测量的随机性与经典格子气体算法内在随机性之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解线性和非线性偏微分方程的量子算法大多依赖非酉操作，这些操作通常以概率方式实现，需要后选择（postselection），从而增加计算成本并导致时间步无法可靠串联。本文旨在消除这一限制，实现无条件成功的非线性量子模拟。

Method: 利用量子格子气体（quantum lattice gas）算法，建立量子测量随机性（在LCU框架中）与经典格子气体算法内在随机性之间的对应关系。通过这种对应，将经典算法中天然存在的随机性映射为量子测量带来的随机性，从而避免后选择的需要，实现时间步的无条件串联。同时识别了适用于这种时间推进格式的概率性经典算法的一般性特征。

Result: 首次提出Burgers方程的时间步可以无概率失败地串联的量子算法，并额外展示了该方法可推广到其他概率性经典算法的场景。

Conclusion: 量子格子气体方法为非线性PDE的量子模拟提供了一种无需后选择、无条件成功的路径，并揭示了量子测量随机性可被利用而非被视为障碍的普遍原理。

Abstract: Most recently proposed quantum algorithms for solving linear and nonlinear partial differential equations rely on non-unitary operations. These operations are typically implemented probabilistically, requiring postselection and thus increasing the computational cost. We show that quantum lattice gas algorithms enable unconditionally successful quantum simulation of nonlinearities, yielding, to our knowledge, the first quantum algorithm for Burgers equation whose time steps can be concatenated without probabilistic failure. The key idea is to exploit the correspondence between the stochasticity of quantum measurement in the linear combination of unitaries framework and the intrinsic randomness of the classical lattice gas algorithm. In doing so, we identify general properties that characterize probabilistic classical algorithms amenable to this time-marching formulation, and illustrate the approach with an additional application.

</details>


### [111] [A Standard Quantum Mechanical Treatment to Rationalize the Delayed-Choice Quantum Eraser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02185)
*Vipul Badhan,Pranvi,Samreet Dhillon,Bindiya Arora*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用标准量子力学框架解决了延迟选择量子擦除实验的悖论，证明该实验中的选择仅仅是测量基的选择，不涉及对光子过去的改写，并指出"反向因果"的错觉源于提出不合法的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自延迟选择量子擦除实验提出并实现以来，其解释和含义一直是基础物理学激烈争论的主题，本文旨在用标准量子力学消解该实验中表象上的悖论。

Method: 采用扩展的Mach-Zehnder干涉仪复现原始实验的每一个操作特征；将该实验映射到双路Stern-Gerlach框架中以证明量子擦除是测量纠缠态的预期结果；通过教学性博弈/游戏阐明"反向因果"错觉的来源。

Result: 证明在"路径探测器"与"擦除探测器"之间做选择仅仅是测量基的选择，并不重写光子的过去；量子擦除被证明是测量纠缠态的自然结果而非物理异常；前向时间的描述完全足以解释实验逻辑。

Conclusion: 延迟选择实验的悖论可以完全在标准量子力学框架内被消解，"反向因果"的错觉源于对系统提出了不合法的（超出量子力学适用范围的）问题，而非任何真实的随时间回溯的物理过程。

Abstract: Ever since the proposal of delayed choice quantum erasure and subsequent realization in the experiment by Kim et al., the interpretation and implications of delayed-choice experiments have remained a subject of intense foundational debate. This paper resolves the apparent paradox attached to the experiment using standard quantum mechanics. Using an extended Mach-Zehnder interferometer which captures every operational feature of the original experiment, we show that choosing between which-path and erasure detectors is simply a choice of measurement bases, which does not rewrite a photon's past. Furthermore, by mapping the experiment to a two-way Stern-Gerlach framework, we prove that quantum erasure is an expected result of measuring entangled states, not a physical anomaly. Ultimately, through a pedagogical game, we illustrate that the illusion of retrocausality arises from asking illegitimate questions, and that a forward-in-time description is entirely sufficient to explain the logic.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum computer-based simulation of Stark many-body localization in a 1D Fermi-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02245)
*Abdul Kalam,Prasenjit Deb,Akitada Sakurai,Tapan Mishra,V. S. Prasannaa,B. P. Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM超导量子计算机上，通过哈密顿模拟研究了12量子比特费米-哈伯德模型中的Stark多体局域化（MBL）现象。通过结合自旋分辨Jordan-Wigner变换、SWAP网络和张量网络量子电路优化等一系列编译手段，实现了约88%的双量子比特门数量减少和87%的电路深度减少，从而在含噪硬件上成功展示了从弱倾斜下的热化动力学到强倾斜下局域化行为的交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域化（MBL）是非平衡量子多体系统的重要动力学现象，与传统热化不同，它导致系统对初始状态的长期记忆和纠缠的缓慢增长。在经典计算机上模拟MBL存在指数复杂性瓶颈，而真实量子硬件为研究该现象提供了潜在优势。然而当前含噪量子计算设备的限制使得实现此类模拟面临挑战，因此需要有效的电路编译优化策略。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1)使用自旋分辨的Jordan-Wigner变换对一维费米-哈伯德模型进行编码；(2)采用SWAP网络实现量子比特间的长程相互作用；(3)在标准电路优化流水线之上集成基于张量网络的量子电路优化例程；(4)使用Trotter化的量子电路模拟实时间动力学。通过这些编译步骤组合，大幅降低了电路复杂度和深度。

Result: 编译优化后，双量子比特门数量减少了约88%，电路深度减少了约87%。在12量子比特费米-哈伯德系统中，通过实时动力学模拟观察到：在弱倾斜场下系统呈现热化行为，而在大倾斜场和短演化时间下系统表现出强局域化行为，并展示了从热化到局域化的交叉现象。结果与精确模拟结果进行了基准对比。

Conclusion: 该工作成功地在当前含噪IBM超导量子计算机上演示了Stark MBL现象的量子模拟，证明了通过系统的电路编译优化策略可以在现有量子硬件上研究复杂的量子多体动力学现象。这一成果为在近期量子设备上进一步探索多体局域化和相关非平衡量子现象奠定了基础。

Abstract: Many-body localization (MBL) is a dynamical phenomenon that describes the non-ergodicity of isolated quantum many-body systems. In contrast to thermalization, this phenomenon leads to a long-lived memory of initial states of local systems and slow growth of entanglement. In this work, we study Stark MBL in a 12-qubit correlated fermionic system described by the one-dimensional Fermi-Hubbard model using Hamiltonian simulation on an IBM superconducting qubit quantum computer. To enable such a computation on current-day noisy hardware, we combine a series of compilation steps, including the use of the spin-resolved Jordan-Wigner transformation, employing SWAP networks, and integrating a tensor-network-based quantum circuit optimization routine on top of a standard circuit optimization pipeline. As a result, there is approximately an 88$\%$ and 87$\%$ reduction in two-qubit gate count and circuit depth, respectively. Through such simulations of the real-time dynamics using Trotterized quantum circuits, we exhibit a crossover from thermalizing dynamics of the system at a weak tilt of the field to a strongly localized behavior at large tilt with short evolution times. We also benchmark our obtained results with respect to those from exact simulations.

</details>


### [113] [Effects of realistic pulse shapes in two-dimensional spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02275)
*M. Russo,R. Gilliot,A. Blech,M. Joffre,C. P. Koch,H. Seiler,Département de Physique,Institut Polytechnique de Paris,Palaiseau,France,Laboratoire d'Optique et Biosciences,CNRS,Inserm,École Polytechnique,Institut Polytechnique de Paris,Palaiseau,France)*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的二维光谱模拟方法，适用于马尔可夫环境极限，能够处理任意脉冲形状并重现时间排序和重叠脉冲效应，计算成本随采样点数线性增长。通过该方法研究了光谱相位畸变和非高斯脉冲形状（如空心光纤或非共线光学放大器产生的脉冲）对二维光谱的影响，发现真实脉冲会引入额外光谱特征、线形畸变和振荡贡献，且即使微弱的脉冲时间尾部也会导致二维光谱的可见变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维光谱是揭示量子态耦合和解析系统光学响应各贡献的强大技术，但现有模拟方法在处理真实实验中产生的非理想脉冲形状（如光谱相位畸变和高度非高斯脉冲）时存在局限性，这可能导致实验结果解读的歧义。

Method: 提出一种在马尔可夫环境极限下进行二维光谱模拟的高效方法，该方法支持任意脉冲形状、能重现时间排序和脉冲重叠效应，且计算成本与采样点数呈线性关系。利用该框架系统研究光谱相位畸变和高度非高斯脉冲形状对二维光谱的影响，并考察外差探测方案对脉冲效应的缓解作用。

Result: 研究发现真实脉冲可导致二维光谱中出现额外光谱特征、线形畸变和系统动力学中的振荡贡献。即使是未校正高阶光谱相位引起的微弱时间脉冲尾部，也会引起二维光谱的可见变化。此外，实验中采用的外差探测方案能够在一定程度上缓解这些脉冲效应带来的影响。

Conclusion: 在二维光谱模拟中纳入真实脉冲形状至关重要，有助于识别脉冲诱导效应并最大限度地减少实验数据解读中的歧义。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) spectroscopy is a powerful pump-pump-probe technique for revealing couplings between quantum states and disentangling the different contributions to the optical response of a system. We present an efficient method for 2D spectroscopy simulations in the Markovian limit for the environment, capable of handling arbitrary pulse shapes and reproducing time-ordering and overlapping pulse effects, while maintaining a computational cost that scales linearly with the number of sampling points. We leverage this framework to investigate how 2D spectra are affected by spectral phase distortions and highly non-Gaussian pulse shapes, such as those produced experimentally by hollow-core fibers or non-collinear optical amplifiers. We show that realistic pulses can induce the appearance of additional spectral features, lineshape distortions and oscillating contributions in the system's dynamics. Notably, even weak temporal pulse tails arising from uncorrected high-order spectral phase terms cause visible changes in the 2D spectra. We also find that homodyne detection schemes employed in experiments can mitigate the presence of such pulse effects. These results emphasize the importance of including realistic pulses in 2D spectroscopy simulations to identify pulse-induced effects and minimize ambiguities in the interpretation of experimental data.

</details>


### [114] [Learnable yet not simulable: a quantum resource theory of learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02325)
*Xinbiao Wang,Yuxuan Du,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的量子资源度量——动力学稳定子熵（DSE），用于量化可调量子电路期望值函数在频率模式上的分布广度，并建立了将经典模拟器与量子数据辅助经典替代模型进行比较的计算相图，从而界定了经典模拟与经典学习之间的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子资源理论已阐明了量子系统的经典可模拟性，但哪个量子资源决定量子电路的经典可学习性仍不明确，尤其是在经典高效模拟失效的区域之外。本文旨在填补这一知识空白，建立量子资源与学习性之间的定量联系。

Method: 本文引入新的资源度量——动力学稳定子熵（DSE），将其与算子稳定子熵建立联系；通过对经典替代模型的样本复杂度和运行时间推导边界来刻画学习性边界；开发了DSE引导的经典替代模型；并在标准复杂性理论假设下证明存在可被该替代模型高效学习但无法从电路描述高效模拟的电路族；通过多达80量子比特的随机和结构化电路数值实验进行验证。

Result: 建立了DSE依赖的计算相图，确定了经典学习与经典模拟的分界；证明了存在可被高效学习但不可高效模拟的电路族；数值实验支持预测的DSE依赖计算景观。

Conclusion: 本文建立了量化资源理论框架，用于划分经典模拟与经典学习的边界，提出了连接量子资源与可学习性的资源度量，并为超出直接经典模拟范围的扩展量子系统设计基于学习的算法提供了指导。

Abstract: Quantum resource theory has sharpened our understanding of the intrinsic complexity of quantum systems, particularly their classical simulability. However, it remains unclear which quantum resource governs the classical learnability of quantum circuits, especially beyond the regime of efficient classical simulation. Here we close this knowledge gap by studying the expectation-value functions of families of tunable quantum circuits, with many applications in digital quantum simulation, quantum metrology, and quantum-system characterization. Specifically, we introduce a new resource measure, the dynamical stabilizer entropy (\DSE), which quantifies how broadly an expectation-value function is distributed across its frequency modes. By relating \DSE to operator stabilizer entropy, we establish a computational phase diagram that compares classical simulators with quantum-data-assisted classical surrogates. We first determine the \DSE-dependent learnability boundary of this diagram by deriving bounds on the sample complexity and runtime of classical surrogates, and by developing a \DSE-guided surrogate. We then complete the diagram by proving, under standard complexity-theoretic assumptions, the existence of circuit families that can be efficiently learned by this surrogate but cannot be efficiently emulated from their circuit descriptions alone. Numerical experiments on random and structured circuits with up to 80 qubits support the predicted \DSE-dependent computational landscape. These results establish a quantitative resource-theoretic framework for delineating the boundary between classical simulation and learning, motivate resource measures linking quantum resources to learnability, and guide the design of learning-based algorithms for scalable quantum systems beyond the reach of direct classical simulation.

</details>


### [115] [Finite-Syndrome Compression of Quantum Fisher Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02333)
*Jianqi Sheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子传感中可读错误记录（readable error records）在有限伴生系统（finite ancilla）约束下的最优压缩问题。作者证明了精细与粗糙对称对数导数量子Fisher信息（SLD QFI）之间丢失量的精确恒等式，将最优有限综合征设计转化为算子值聚类问题，并给出零损失的条件。此外，论文还从图论角度建立了精确恢复量子码所需最少可读综合征数与Knill-Laflamme不相容图色数的联系，并对具体噪声模型给出了闭合形式的最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子传感中，可读的错误记录有助于防止不同噪声轨迹被不可逆地混合，从而保护传感性能。然而实际中有限的探测器或辅助系统只能保留有限多数量的综合征值（syndrome values），目前缺乏一个通用判据来确定哪些记录可以合并而不会丢失计量学信息。这一空白阻碍了在有限资源约束下设计最优的量子传感方案。

Method: 本文首先将问题形式化为固定细粒度经典-量子记录在参数无关压缩下至多M个标志（flags）的数学模型。核心方法包括：1) 证明丢失的SLD QFI精确等于精细与粗SLD得分之间状态加权平方距离之和，从而将最优有限综合征设计转化为算子值聚类问题；2) 将该恒等式扩展到完整多参数SLD QFI矩阵；3) 针对精确恢复量子码的情形，证明最小可读综合征数等于Knill-Laflamme不相容图的色数；4) 对qubit随机酉噪声推导有限划分公式，并对平面随机-Pauli模型给出条件方差表示及精确最优解。

Result: 获得了丢失SLD QFI的精确恒等式；建立了零损失的支持分解共同SLD条件；区分了局部QFI保持与整个统计模型恢复；得到了量子码精确恢复所需的色数刻画；对qubit随机酉噪声获得有限划分公式；对均匀误差轴的平面随机Pauli模型得到闭式最优值F_M*=[M sin(π/M)/π]^2，亏缺为π²/(3M²)+O(M⁻⁴)。

Conclusion: 本文精确刻画了有限综合征分辨率的信息论代价，明确了被动噪声到擦除解释所需的边信息假设，为量子传感中有限资源下的错误记录压缩与综合征设计提供了系统性的理论框架。

Abstract: Readable error records can protect quantum sensing because they prevent physically distinct noise trajectories from being irreversibly mixed. A finite detector or ancilla, however, can retain only finitely many syndrome values, and a general criterion for deciding which records may be merged without losing metrological information is absent. We formulate the problem for a fixed fine-grained classical-quantum record and parameter-independent compression into at most $M$ flags. We prove an exact identity expressing the lost symmetric-logarithmic-derivative quantum Fisher information (SLD QFI) as a sum of state-weighted squared distances between fine and coarse SLD scores. Consequently, optimal finite-syndrome design is exactly an operator-valued clustering problem, and zero loss is characterized by a support-resolved common-SLD condition. We extend the identity to the full multiparameter SLD QFI matrix and distinguish local QFI preservation from recovery of an entire statistical model. For exact recovery of a quantum code, we separately show that, when each fine error is individually correctable, the minimum number of readable syndromes is the chromatic number of a Knill-Laflamme incompatibility graph. For qubit random-unitary noise we obtain a finite partition formula. A planar random-Pauli model admits a conditional-variance representation and, for a uniform error axis, the exact optimum $F_M^{\star}=[M\sin(π/M)/π]^2$, with deficit $π^2/(3M^2)+O(M^{-4})$. These results identify the information-theoretic cost of finite syndrome resolution while making explicit the side-information assumptions required for any passive noise-to-erasure interpretation.

</details>


### [116] [Revealing and reducing growth-induced interfacial disorder in preferentially aligned nitrogen-vacancy centers in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02350)
*Cheng-I Ho,Marina Davydova,Patrik Straňák,Felix Hoffmann,Peter Knittel,Andrej Denisenko,Jörg Wrachtrup*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了化学气相沉积（CVD）金刚石中氮-空位（NV）色心层在氮注入过程中产生的界面无序问题。研究发现，脉冲式氮注入会在60–80 nm界面区域引发显著的氮过冲和过多缺陷；而通过质量流量控制器平滑供氮则可大幅抑制界面无序，使相干性质接近自旋浴噪声所限的理论极限。作者用50 nm NV层演示了质子核磁共振检测，为生长高质量薄NV掺杂层提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVD金刚石中优先取向的NV色心薄膜是多种量子成像技术的基石，但氮注入过程会瞬时驱动生长偏离平衡，产生界面应变和自旋缺陷，严重降低NV相干性。理解并抑制这一界面无序对制备高性能、低缺陷密度的薄NV层至关重要。

Method: 在(111)金刚石上生长$^{12}$C富集的优先取向NV层，采用两种氮注入方法（脉冲注入 vs. 质量流量控制器平滑输送），结合纳米级选择性等离子体刻蚀和NV自旋相干性测量，系统表征界面区域的无序程度及其对相干性的影响。

Result: 脉冲氮注入在60–80 nm界面区产生明显氮过冲和过量缺陷；而平滑氮输送显著抑制界面无序，NV相干性接近自旋浴噪声决定的理论极限。50 nm NV层成功用于质子核磁共振检测。

Conclusion: 氮掺杂方式直接决定了NV层界面无序的严重程度，平滑氮输送是制备高质量、薄层NV掺杂金刚石的有效策略，为量子传感应用提供了可靠生长路线。

Abstract: Nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in chemical-vapor-deposition (CVD) diamond can form preferentially oriented ensembles with high sensing performance and low densities of lattice defects. Thin films of this material are a cornerstone of various imaging modalities. However, nitrogen injection needed to produce such films can transiently drive growth out of equilibrium, generating interfacial strain and spin defects that degrade NV coherence. Here, we investigate this disorder in $^{12}\text{C}$-enriched, preferentially oriented NV layers grown on (111) diamond using two nitrogen-injection procedures, combined with nanometer-scale selective plasma etching and NV spin-coherence measurements. Pulsed nitrogen injection produces a pronounced nitrogen overshoot within a 60--80 nm interfacial region, generating excessive amounts of defects. By contrast, smooth nitrogen delivery through mass flow controllers substantially suppresses interfacial disorder, yielding coherence properties close to the theoretical limit imposed by spin-bath noise. A 50-nm NV layer is used to demonstrate proton nuclear magnetic resonance detection. This work reveals the role of interfacial disorder associated with the nitrogen-doping procedure and provides a route to growing high-quality, thin NV-doped layers for quantum-sensing applications.

</details>


### [117] [Entanglement Generation Beyond Quantum Theory: From Product States to Popescu-Rohrlich Boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02403)
*Shun Umekawa,Akihiro Hokkyo,Hayato Arai,Kazuaki Takasan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文首次在boxworld广义概率理论中构造了保持纯态（非可逆）的变换，能够将任意无关联的纯态映射为Popescu-Rohrlich盒，并完整分类了最简单二分boxworld中所有保持纯态的纠缠生成变换，证明超出量子力学的纠缠生成无需引入混合即可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论中纠缠可通过酉动力学生成，但在boxworld中，可逆变换无法生成纠缠。作者质疑这一结论是否依赖于"可逆性"这一过强约束，并探究当放宽为更弱的"纯态保持"条件时，boxworld是否能够生成纠缠。这也旨在揭示量子理论中因结构特殊性而被掩盖的"可逆性"与"纯态保持"之间的物理区别。

Method: 作者在boxworld（允许Popescu-Rohrlich盒的广义概率理论框架）中，将变换约束从可逆性放宽为纯态保持，构造了一个能够将任意无关联纯态映射为PR盒的显式变换，并对最简单（二分）boxworld中所有纯态保持的纠缠生成变换进行了完整分类。

Result: 证明了在boxworld中，非可逆但保持纯态的变换确实能够生成纠缠（包括PR盒级别的超量子关联），并给出了首个不引入混合即可生成超量子纠缠的显式机制。同时完成了二分boxworld中此类变换的完全分类。

Conclusion: 在boxworld中，可逆性与纯态保持是物理上不同的两个概念，二者在量子理论中被其特殊结构所遮蔽。纯态保持变换为生成超量子纠缠提供了可行途径，为广义概率理论中纠缠资源的动力学生成开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Entanglement generation is a fundamental dynamical capability in quantum information science and underpins many quantum advantages. While quantum theory enables it through unitary dynamics, boxworld, a generalized probabilistic theory admitting Popescu--Rohrlich boxes with supraquantum correlations, has no reversible transformation capable of generating entanglement. We show that this no-go picture changes fundamentally once reversibility is relaxed to pure-state preservation. We construct a pure-state-preserving transformation that maps every uncorrelated pure state to a Popescu--Rohrlich box and completely classify all pure-state-preserving entangling transformations in the simplest bipartite boxworld. Our results provide the first explicit mechanism for generating beyond-quantum entanglement without introducing mixing and demonstrate a physical distinction between reversibility and pure-state preservation that is obscured by the structure of quantum theory.

</details>


### [118] [Caustics and Superenergy in the Quantum Bouncer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02427)
*Marko. M. Ćosić,Andrew N. Jordan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子弹跳球问题中与经典焦散线相关的量子干涉与能量现象（称为"量子焦散"）。通过初始高斯波包，展示了与经典轨迹族相关的焦散结构，并将其附近的相位奇点链与基于尖点突变线的半经典Pearcey函数联系起来。同时量化了超能量（局部能量超过最大本征能量特征值）的大小，并用Madelung/Bohm轨迹对焦散和超能量行为进行了补充描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解量子系统中经典焦散结构的量子对应物，特别是相位奇点链和超能量现象，以建立经典-量子对应关系的更完整图像。

Method: 采用初始高斯波包研究量子弹跳球问题，使用半经典Pearcey函数（基于尖点突变线）分析焦散附近的相位奇点链，并通过Madelung/Bohm轨迹提供补充性描述。

Result: 展示了经典轨迹族的焦散在量子波包中得以呈现，将相位奇点链与Pearcey函数建立了联系，并量化了超能量区域的大小；Madelung/Bohm轨迹分析揭示了轨迹能量及其穿越相位奇点链的行为。

Conclusion: 量子焦散为理解经典焦散的量子对应提供了统一框架，Pearcey函数和Bohm轨迹共同为相位奇点链和超能量现象提供了互补且深入的见解。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum interference and energetic phenomena associated with classical caustics in the quantum bouncing ball problem, we refer to as quantum caustics. By considering an initial Gaussian wavepacket, we show that caustics associated with the underlying classical trajectory families are exhibited. We connect the associated phase singularity chains in the vicinity of the caustic with the semiclassical Pearcey function built on the cusp catastrophe lines. We also quantify the amount of superenergy exhibited in these solutions - regions of space where the local energy exceeds the largest constituent energy eigenvalue. We give a complimentary description of the caustic and superenergy behavior using the Madelung/Bohm trajectories, which gives additional insight about the energy of the trajectories and how they traverse the phase singularity chains.

</details>


### [119] [Detecting high-dimensional entanglement with simple measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02439)
*Suraj Goel,Alexander Bernal,Gabriele Cobucci,Will McCutcheon,Mehul Malik,Armin Tavakoli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅基于单量子比特观测序列来检测高维纠缠Schmidt数的方案，无需实现昂贵的局部基测量。通过多平面光转换技术，在16维光子系统上实验验证了该方法能够检测到最大（或接近最大）的Schmidt数，显著简化了实验设置的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维纠缠的标准基准是Schmidt数，其检测通常需要实现适当的局部基测量。然而随着量子技术扩展到越来越大的物理维度，实现这类测量的成本通常变得更高。因此需要一种更简单、更可扩展的方案来检测高维纠缠性质。

Method: 提出了一种仅基于序列单量子比特可观测量的Schmidt数检测方案。这些测量实现更简单，只需要低深度量子电路。实验上使用多平面光转换技术，基于16维光子空间模式纠缠进行验证。

Result: 实验成功检测到最大（或接近最大）的Schmidt数，证实了简单且更可扩展的测量足以检测高维纠缠特性，同时显著降低了设置复杂度。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了简单、可扩展的测量方案足以有效检测高维纠缠特性，为未来更大维度量子系统下的纠缠检测提供了一种实用且低成本的途径。

Abstract: The standard benchmark for high-dimensional entanglement is the number of dimensions in which entanglement must be present in order to generate the state. This is called the Schmidt number and its detection is usually based on implementing an appropriate set of local basis measurements. However, as quantum technology brings increasingly large physical dimensions within reach, the implementation of such measurements typically becomes more costly. Here, we develop a scheme for detecting Schmidt numbers based only on sequences of single-qubit observables. These measurements are simpler to implement as they require only low-depth quantum circuits. Using up to sixteen-dimensional photonic spatial mode entanglement and multi-plane light conversion technology, we demonstrate how it simplifies setup complexity and successfully detects the maximal (or close-to-maximal) Schmidt number. Our results reveal that simple and more scalable measurements are sufficient to detect high-dimensional entanglement properties.

</details>


### [120] [Nanohertz Pendulum toward Macroscopic Entanglement under Structural Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02462)
*Azusa Sawada,Hina Nakano,Kanta Watanabe,Gaku Ohashi,Shota Okumura,Nobuyuki Matsumoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了用于宏观量子控制的钟摆系统，指出结构阻尼导致的1/f力噪声谱中的低频尾部会损害纠缠性能（在10%探测损耗下所需的反作用力与热力噪声比提高约50%）。研究者通过阶梯熔石英光纤悬挂7mg钟摆（Q=7.3×10^6），将ω₀Γ降至约2.63Hz×361nHz，实现了G_q≈2.5的增益，超过所需G_req=1.49的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钟摆因重力稀释降低机械损耗而适用于宏观量子控制，但结构阻尼的1/f噪声谱存在低频尾部会惩罚纠缠。需要解决这一结构性噪声缺陷，提高量子控制性能。

Method: 通过理论分析1/f噪声谱对纠缠的影响（10%探测损耗下反作用力与热力噪声比需提高50%），设计并实现了一个7mg钟摆，采用阶梯状熔石英光纤悬挂（ω₀/2π=2.63 Hz），测量能量衰减率Γ/2π=361(39) nHz，对应Q=7.3(8)×10^6。

Result: 测量得到的增益G_q≈2.5，相对于之前的单片器件有了显著提升，超过了所需增益G_req=1.49的要求，成功克服了结构噪声带来的性能惩罚。

Conclusion: 通过降低ω₀Γ（谐振频率与能量衰减率之积），可以有效抑制结构阻尼1/f噪声低频尾部的负面影响，使钟摆系统达到宏观量子控制所需的性能指标。

Abstract: Pendulums are attractive for macroscopic quantum control because gravity dilution reduces mechanical loss, while the $1/f$ force-noise spectrum associated with structural damping allows nearly lossless trapping to suppress the thermal noise sampled at an upward-shifted resonance. The same $1/f$ spectrum, however, produces a low-frequency tail that penalizes entanglement. With $10\%$ detection loss, we find that this tail raises the required back-action-to-thermal force-noise ratio by about $50\%$, corresponding to a required suspension gain $G_{\rm req}=1.49$. To overcome this structural-noise penalty, we realize a $7$-mg pendulum suspended by a stepped fused-silica fiber, with an energy-decay rate $Γ/2π=361(39)$ nHz ($Q\equivω_0/Γ=7.3(8)\times10^6$) at $ω_0/2π=2.63$ Hz. The reduction in $ω_0Γ$ yields a measured gain $G_q\simeq2.5$ relative to the previous monolithic device, exceeding the requirement.

</details>


### [121] [High-dimensional quantum process tomography with undetected photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02490)
*Salini Rajeev,Mayukh Lahiri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于干涉测量的量子过程层析成像方法，通过利用高维量子态（qudit），可以在不对变换后的qudit进行任何测量的情况下，完整重建任意量子操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子过程层析成像必须对变换后的qudit进行测量才能完整表征量子操作，这限制了其实际应用。作者希望通过一种概念上不同的干涉测量方法，避免对变换后量子态的直接测量，从而实现对任意量子操作的完整重建。

Method: 该方法利用高维量子态（qudit）和干涉测量的原理。与现有技术不同，该方法不需要对变换后的qudit进行测量，而是通过干涉效应提取操作的全部信息，从而实现对任意量子操作的完整层析成像。

Result: 证明了利用高维量子态和干涉测量方法，可以在不测量变换后qudit的情况下完整重建任意量子操作，且该方法在概念上区别于现有的量子过程层析成像技术。

Conclusion: 作者展示了一种全新的、干涉测量式的量子过程层析成像方案，它借助高维量子态，免除了对变换后量子态的测量需求，为量子过程表征提供了新的途径。

Abstract: The goal of quantum process tomography is to fully characterize an operation performed on a quantum state. By considering high-dimensional quantum states (qudit), we show that it is possible to fully reconstruct an arbitrary operation without performing any measurement on the transformed qudit. Our method is interferometric and conceptually different from existing techniques of quantum process tomography that must perform a measurement on the transformed qudit.

</details>


### [122] [Squeezing-Fueled Quantum Otto Engine via Measurement-Induced Cooling: The Two-Qubit Quantum Rabi Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02521)
*S R Rathnakaran,Asoka Biswas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种基于两量子比特量子Rabi模型的量子奥托发动机，运行在腔量子电动力学架构中。该引擎采用非马尔可夫热浴模拟（HEOM），通过腔模的投影测量协议替代冷热库，并利用腔模的压缩驱动作为量子燃料。结果显示腔模压缩可系统性地增强功率和效率，使效率高于标准量子奥托极限，并在极限环区域渐近收敛至奥托界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子热机依赖双热浴（热浴与冷浴），其效率和功率受限于标准量子奥托极限。作者试图探索超越传统双浴架构的新量子热力学设计方案，特别是利用测量诱导冷却和量子压缩等非经典资源来优化热机性能，为实验可实现的量子热机提供新的平台与理论依据。

Method: 采用两量子比特作为工作物质，置于单个非马尔可夫热浴中，通过分层运动方程（HEOM）精确描述非马尔可夫动力学；冷却冲程用腔模上的投影测量协议实现，利用测量反作用模拟冷浴效果；腔模施加压缩驱动作为量子燃料，系统研究压缩强度对输出功和热机效率的影响。

Result: 腔模压缩系统地增强了热机的功率输出和运行效率，使效率超过标准量子奥托极限；在极限循环状态下，效率仍高于奥托界但渐近地（从上方）收敛于该界限。这表明量子比特-腔耦合、测量诱导冷却与非平衡压缩的协同作用，可实现传统双浴量子奥托热机无法达到的热力学性能。

Conclusion: 压缩态腔场被确认为一种可控的量子资源，可用于热力学优化。该工作提出了一种多资源协同的量子热力学架构，为在腔QED平台上实验实现高性能量子热机提供了具体途径，并拓展了对量子热机设计中非平衡量子资源利用的理解。

Abstract: We investigate a quantum Otto engine (QOE) constructed from the two-qubit quantum Rabi model, operating within a cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED) architecture. The engine operates with two qubits as the working substance and a single non-Markovian hot thermal bath, modeled via the hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) formalism. In place of a conventional cold thermal reservoir, the cooling stroke is realized through a projective measurement protocol on the cavity mode, which acts as an ancillary subsystem and effectively mimics a cold bath for the qubit working medium via measurement back-action. A squeezing drive applied to the cavity mode serves as a quantum fuel. We demonstrate that cavity squeezing systematically enhances both the power output and operational efficiency of the engine - the work extracted per unit of heat drawn from the hot bath-driving it above the standard quantum Otto limit. In the limit-cycle regime, the efficiency, while remaining above the Otto bound throughout, asymptotically converges to it from above. This identifies squeezing as a controllable quantum resource for thermodynamic optimization. Our results reveal that the interplay between qubit-cavity coupling, measurement-induced cooling, and non-equilibrium squeezing gives rise to a multi-resource thermodynamic architecture with performance characteristics inaccessible to conventional two-bath quantum Otto engines, thereby providing a concrete route toward experimentally realizable quantum heat engines in cavity QED platforms.

</details>


### [123] [Entanglement of flower states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02587)
*Samrat Sen,Ludovico Lami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了"花态"（flower states）这一纠缠态家族，计算了其主要纠缠度量，发现了NE操作下最大的不可逆性间隙（与局部维度d的1/2 log d量级相关），并证明了squashed纠缠在NE操作下不是单调量。此外还精确计算了LOCC操作下的纠缠成本（由Schmidt数给出），利用Tao和Meshulam关于循环群的不确定性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠态的操作结构极其复杂，存在不同层次的自由操作类（如LOCC、NE等），导致纠缠操纵在不同级别上表现出不同的效能。理解这些操作类之间的差异，特别是不可逆性间隙（蒸馏纠缠与纠缠成本之差），是量子信息理论的核心问题。花态提供了一个自然的参数化族来揭示这一景观。

Method: 作者以局部维度为偶数2k的花态为研究对象，系统计算了所有主要的纠缠度量。具体方法包括：利用tempered纠缠负性（tempered entanglement negativity）证明NE操作下花态的纠缠成本；通过Tao和Meshulam关于循环群的Heisenberg-Weyl不确定性关系来计算LOCC操作下的精确纠缠成本（即Schmidt数）。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 花态的蒸馏纠缠在所有形式下均为1 ebit，与局部维度无关；(2) LOCC下的纠缠成本为log(2√k)，而NE下的成本为log(1+√k)，仅比LOCC少约1 ebit，形成NE操作下最大的已知不可逆性间隙Θ(1/2 log d)；(3) squashed纠缠在NE下不是单调量；(4) LOCC下的精确成本由Schmidt数给出，等于min_{r|k} log(r + k/r)，对素数k简化为log(k+1)，约为标准LOCC成本的两倍。

Conclusion: 该工作通过花态揭示了不同纠缠操作类之间的深刻差异，特别是NE与LOCC之间在纠缠操纵能力上仍然存在显著差距；同时展示了量子信息论中纠缠度量（如squashed纠缠）的局限性，以及不确定性原理与纠缠成本之间的深层联系。

Abstract: The mysterious nature of entanglement, one of the most prominent exquisitely quantum phenomena, is reflected in its intricate operational structure, with a hierarchy of classes of free operations that enable its manipulation at different levels of effectiveness. Here we use the class of 'flower states', parametrised by their (even) local dimension $2k$, to shine light on some aspects of this varied landscape. We compute all the main entanglement measures for flower states, uncovering a large gap between all forms of distillable entanglement, equal to 1 ebit independently of the local dimension, and the entanglement cost under local operations and classical communication (LOCC), known to be equal to $\log\big(2\sqrt{k}\big)$. Even under the strictly more powerful class of non-entangling (NE) operations, we show that their cost is still equal to $\log\big(1+\sqrt{k}\big)$, only about an ebit less than for LOCCs. This result, which we prove by calculating the recently introduced tempered entanglement negativity for these states, demonstrates the largest known 'irreversibility gap', i.e. the difference between distillable entanglement and entanglement cost, under NE operations, equal to $Θ\big(\frac12 \log d\big)$, with $d$ being the local dimension. A notable consequence is that the celebrated squashed entanglement is not a monotone under NE operations. Finally, we compute the exact cost under LOCC operations for flower states; this is given by the Schmidt number, which turns out to be additive over multiple copies and equal to $\min_{r|k} \log\left( r + \frac{k}{r} \right)$; for prime $k$ this reduces to $\log(k+1)$, about twice the standard LOCC cost. These last results leverage the uncertainty relations over cyclic groups proved by Tao and Meshulam.

</details>


### [124] [Optimal Quantum de Finetti Theorems via Argmax Rounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02590)
*Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Pei Wu,Haochen Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了最优的有限量子 de Finetti 上界：对于玻色态，二阶边际的 1-范数误差至多为 √(d−1)/(N−1)；通过纯化，任意可交换态得到最优的 O(d/N) 上界。该结果解决了 Christandl 等人（CMP 2007）遗留的维度依赖问题，并进一步推导出 Watrous 解纠缠猜想（disentangler conjecture）的反例，以及显式 Best Separable State 算法和可分离性测试的确定性算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限量子 de Finetti 定理的维度依赖上界此前未被完全解决，其最优形式对量子信息理论（如纠缠检测、量子密码学、可分离性判定等）具有重要意义。Watrous 的解纠缠猜想是否成立也与该上界紧密相关，亟需确定紧致的维度依赖关系。

Method: 本文将 de Finetti 近似重新表述为对称扩展半正定规划（symmetric-extension SDP）的积分间隙问题，并采用 Jeronimo、Wu 和 Xu 的 argmax 方法对最优解进行舍入（sum-of-squares rounding）。对于玻色态，直接获得 √(d−1)/(N−1) 的最优上界；通过纯化推广到一般可交换态；利用谱截断（spectral truncation）技巧在 Hilbert–Schmidt 距离下得到维度无关的玻色型 de Finetti 定理。

Result: (1) 玻色态二阶边际最优上界 √(d−1)/(N−1)，可交换态最优上界 O(d/N)。(2) t-位边际满足 O(t√d/N)（玻色）和 O(td/N)（置换不变）上界。(3) 对任意 ε∈(0,1)，构造了输入维度 D=exp(o(d)) 的通道，其输出 ε-接近局部维度 d 的可分离态，且像集包含所有此类可分离态，推翻 Watrous 解纠缠猜想。(4) 得到确定性 exp(Õ(√d/ε)) 时间的显式 Best Separable State 算法（无完美完备性）和迹距离可分离性测试算法。(5) Hilbert–Schmidt 距离下的维度无关玻色 de Finetti 定理，速率为最优的 Θ(N^{−1/2})。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了有限量子 de Finetti 定理的维度依赖问题，给出的上界在渐近意义下是最优的。该结果不仅否定了 Watrous 的解纠缠猜想，还带来了可分离性与纠缠检测方面的高效确定性算法，并为量子信息理论中的 de Finetti 型定理建立了新的分析框架（SDP 积分间隙 + argmax 舍入）。

Abstract: We prove optimal finite quantum de Finetti upper bounds. Given a bosonic state $ρ_N\in D(\mathrm{Sym}^N(\mathbb C^d))$, there is a probability measure $ν$ on the unit sphere such that \[
  \left\|
  ρ_N^{(2)}-\int |u\rangle\langle u|^{\otimes 2}\,dν(u)
  \right\|_1
  \le \frac{\sqrt{d-1}}{N-1}. \] By purification, the bosonic theorem also gives the optimal $O(d/N)$ upper bound for arbitrary exchangeable states. These results settle the dimension dependence left open by Christandl, König, Mitchison, and Renner (CMP 2007). The proof casts de Finetti approximation as sum-of-squares rounding and applies the argmax method of Jeronimo, Wu, and Xu (manuscript 2026).
  More generally, $t$-site marginals satisfy $O(t\sqrt d/N)$ bosonic and $O(td/N)$ permutation-invariant bounds. Our proof formulates de Finetti approximation as the integrality gap of a symmetric-extension semidefinite program and rounds an optimum by the argmax principle. The sharp bounds have several consequences. For every fixed $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$, we construct a channel with input dimension $D=\exp(O_\varepsilon(\sqrt d\log d))=\exp(o(d))$ whose outputs are $\varepsilon$-close to separable states of local dimension $d$ and whose image contains every such separable state, thereby refuting Watrous's disentangler conjecture. We also obtain deterministic $\exp(\widetilde O(\sqrt d/\varepsilon))$-time algorithms for explicit Best Separable State without perfect completeness and for trace-distance separability testing.
  Finally, spectral truncation gives the first dimension-free bosonic de Finetti theorem in Hilbert--Schmidt distance, with the optimal rate $Θ(N^{-1/2})$ when the dimension may grow.

</details>


### [125] [Nearly tight lower bounds for estimating quantum functionals: Uhlmann fidelity, trace distance, and von Neumann entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02600)
*Qisheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的框架，用于证明估计量子态泛函的下界，解决了多个开放问题，确立了与已知上界匹配的下界：估计Uhlmann保真度、迹距离和冯·诺依曼熵需要Ω̃(N²)个样本，并通过量子样本到查询的提取得出Ω̃(N)的查询下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计量子态泛函（如Uhlmann保真度、迹距离和冯·诺依曼熵）的下界此前是开放问题，缺少一个统一的框架来证明这些下界，从而无法确认自2016年以来众多量子算法的最优性。

Method: 提出一个统一框架来证明估计量子态泛函的下界，该框架可同时处理多个泛函；并利用量子样本到查询（sample-to-query）的提升技术，将样本复杂度下界直接转化为查询复杂度下界。

Result: 证明了估计Uhlmann保真度、迹距离和冯·诺依曼熵需要Ω̃(N²)个样本，并推导出相应的Ω̃(N)查询下界，这些下界与已知上界匹配，从而解决了多个开放问题。

Conclusion: 这些匹配的下界证明了自2016年以来的约十几个量子算法在样本复杂度和查询复杂度意义上都是近乎最优的，结束了相关算法最优性的悬而未决状态。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a unified framework for proving lower bounds for estimating functionals of quantum states. We therefore resolve several open problems by establishing lower bounds that match known upper bounds: we show that it requires $\widetildeΩ(N^2)$ samples to estimate the Uhlmann fidelity, trace distance, and von Neumann entropy. Moreover, they immediately imply matching query lower bounds of $\widetildeΩ(N)$ by quantum sample-to-query lifting. These lower bounds imply the near-optimality of a dozen quantum algorithms since 2016.

</details>

### [126] [Ion Flow under an Applied Electric Field in Semiconducting and Metallic Carbon Nanotubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00211)
*J. B. Sokoloff*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文探讨了两种不同实验研究中碳纳米管（CNT）离子输运行为的差异及其理论根源：Li等人的实验表明11nm长、亚纳米直径的金属性和半导体性碳纳米管中钾离子流速几乎相同；而Cui等人的实验表明100μm长、直径2.6-5.4nm的半导体性碳纳米管中离子电导率高于金属性碳纳米管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要理解为什么不同条件下金属性和半导体性碳纳米管的离子输运行为表现出不同甚至相反的差异，探索造成这些实验差异的可能理论机制。

Method: 对两组已有实验测量结果进行对比分析，探讨可能解释实验差异的理论来源，包括碳纳米管电子性质（金属性vs半导体性）对离子输运影响的潜在机制。

Result: 识别出两组实验在碳纳米管长度（11nm vs 100μm）、直径（亚纳米 vs 2.6-5.4nm）、离子种类、实验条件等方面存在显著差异，这些差异可能导致不同的离子输运行为。

Conclusion: 碳纳米管电子性质对离子输运的影响取决于具体实验条件，包括管径、长度和离子种类等因素，需要进行理论分析来统一理解这些不同的实验观测结果。

Abstract: Measurements made by Li, et. al., showed that the flow rates of potassium ions through 11nm long, subnanometer diameter, metallic and semiconducting carbon nanotubes under an applied electric field are almost the same. In contrast, measurements of the electrical conductivity of potassium chloride solution through 100 long metallic and semiconducting carbon nanotubes with diameters between 2.6-5.4nm by Cui, et. al., show that the ionic conductivity in the semiconducting nanotubes is larger than that of the metallic nanotubes. Possible theoretical origins of these differences are explored in this article.

</details>


### [127] [Universal scaling of the Anomalous Hall voltage of magnetic layer with the adjacent conducting layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00385)
*Zhihao Yan,Qianbiao Liu,Zhengxiao Li,Lijun Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了磁性异质结构中反常霍尔电压随非磁性导电层（如正常金属）厚度和电阻率发生普适且显著缩放的现象，并通过输运实验、解析推导和有限元分析确认其机制为磁性层与非磁性层之间的串联电阻效应，与费米面变化或角动量注入无关，同时证明该效应不影响谐波霍尔电压分析的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反常霍尔电压是磁性异质结构中磁化状态及多种自旋电子学效应的关键指示量，理解其在不同层厚度和电阻率下的缩放行为对于准确解读相关自旋电子学现象至关重要，但此前缺乏对这一普适缩放规律的深入认识。

Method: 结合输运实验测量、解析推导以及有限元分析三种手段，系统研究了磁性异质结构中反常霍尔电压与非磁性导电层厚度和电阻率的依赖关系，并通过对照实验排除费米面变化和角动量注入等替代机制。

Result: 确认反常霍尔电压的缩放源于磁性层与非磁性层之间的串联电阻效应；该效应与费米面变化或来自非磁性层的角动量注入无关；同时证明谐波霍尔电压分析精度不受串联电阻效应导致的反常霍尔电压及其他横向电压低估的影响。

Conclusion: 该发现为理解涉及反常霍尔及其他横向电压的多种自旋电子学现象提供了关键信息，有助于更准确地解读相关实验数据。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall voltage of magnetic heterostructures plays a key role as the indicator for the magnetization state and its interplay with a variety of spintronic effects. In this letter, we report the observation, mechanism, and impact of the universal, dramatic scaling of the anomalous Hall voltage of magnetic heterostructures with the thickness and resistivity of the nonmagnetic conducting layers (e.g., normal metal) by combining transport experiments, analytical derivation, and finite-element analyses. We identify that the mechanism is the serial resistor effect of the magnetic and non-magnetic layers within the magnetic heterostructures and irrelevant to any Fermi surface variation or angular momentum injection from the nonmagnetic layer to the magnetic layer. We also show that the accuracy of the harmonic Hall voltage analyses of magnetic heterostructures are unaffected by the under-measuring of the anomalous Hall voltage and other transverse voltages due to the serial resistor effect. These findings provide crucial information for understanding a variety of spintronic phenomena involving the anomalous Hall and other transverse voltages.

</details>


### [128] [Planar Interfaces for Transmission of Chiral Spin Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00388)
*Robin Msiska,Cynthia J. O. Reichhardt,Charles Reichhardt,Eric Fullerton,Avadh Saxena*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过微磁模拟和解析降维判据，系统研究了斯格明子横穿FM-FM、AFM-AFM、FM-AFM和AFM-FM四种平面磁性界面的传输行为，建立了基于接收区形貌的相图，并给出了支持紧凑传输的降维Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交互作用判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向磁性界面为测试斯格明子在跨越材料参数和磁有序突变时是否保持稳健性提供了直接途径，这对于设计异构斯格明子赛道器件至关重要。

Method: 采用微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）和解析降维坐标判据（analytic reduced-coordinate criteria）相结合的方法，研究四类平面界面（FM-FM、AFM-AFM、FM-AFM、AFM-FM）上的斯格明子传输。

Result: 建立了按接收区形貌组织的相图，区分紧凑传输、变形斯格明子、条状畴态、非晶织构和背景弛豫。同序界面（FM-FM、AFM-AFM）的传输由解析定义的降维DMI范围描述，识别出支持紧凑传输的壁软化区域；混合序界面（FM-AFM、AFM-FM）具有方向各异性，需要铁磁磁化与反铁磁Néel织构之间的转换。

Conclusion: 平面界面作为主动传输元件发挥作用，本文结果为异构斯格明子赛道提供了降维设计判据。

Abstract: Lateral magnetic interfaces provide a direct way to test whether skyrmions remain robust when driven across abrupt changes in material parameters and magnetic order. Here we study skyrmion transmission across planar ferromagnet-ferromagnet (FM-FM), antiferromagnet-antiferromagnet (AFM-AFM), ferromagnet-antiferromagnet (FM-AFM), and antiferromagnet-ferromagnet (AFM-FM) interfaces using micromagnetic simulations and analytic reduced-coordinate criteria. The outcomes are organized into phase diagrams according to the morphology formed in the receiving region, distinguishing compact transmission from deformed skyrmions, stripe-domain states, amorphous textures, and relaxation into the background. Same-order FM-FM and AFM-AFM skyrmion transmission is captured by an analytically defined range of the reduced Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, identifying the wall-softening regime that supports compact transmission without stripe formation. Mixed-order FM-AFM and AFM-FM interfaces are directionally distinct, requiring conversion between ferromagnetic magnetization and antiferromagnetic Néel textures. These results show that planar interfaces act as active transport elements and provide reduced design criteria for heterogeneous skyrmion tracks.

</details>


### [129] [Skyrmion Excitations in the $ν=-1$ Quantum Hall state in Monolayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00390)
*Jincheng An,Ajit C. Balram,Ganpathy Murthy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了单层石墨烯在填充因子ν=-1处SU(4)量子霍尔铁磁基态上的斯格明子激发态。通过结合有效场论和Hartree-Fock近似，建立了一个同时纳入长程库仑相互作用和对称性破缺项的变分框架，确定了最优斯格明子纹理，建立了斯格明子激发相图，识别出13种不同的斯格明子相并确定了其自旋-谷纹理、能量和特征尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单层石墨烯的多分量量子霍尔铁磁体系中，斯格明子这类拓扑激发态的研究需要系统性地处理短程各向异性相互作用、塞曼耦合和亚晶格对称性破缺势之间的竞争，以及长程库仑相互作用的影响。

Method: 将有效场论与Hartree-Fock近似相结合，构建变分框架，平等处理长程库仑相互作用与各类对称性破缺项。通过变分最小化确定最优斯格明子纹理（包括空间径向轮廓和内部SU(4)自旋子结构），并针对不同径向ansätze进行鲁棒性验证。

Result: 建立了斯格明子激发相图，确定了外部场条件下的13种不同斯格明子相及其自旋-谷纹理、能量和特征尺寸；结果表明不同径向ansätze下结果具有鲁棒性，验证了变分方法的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究多分量量子霍尔铁磁体中的斯格明子激发提供了系统性框架，可自然推广到更一般的SU(4)量子霍尔系统。

Abstract: We investigate the skyrmion excited states atop the $SU(4)$ quantum Hall ferromagnetic ground state at filling factor $ν=-1$ in monolayer graphene. The competition among short-range anisotropic interactions, the Zeeman coupling, and sublattice symmetry-breaking potential gives rise to four distinct spin-valley-ordered ground-state phases. Combining effective field theory with the Hartree--Fock approximation, we develop a variational framework that incorporates both the long-range Coulomb interaction and the symmetry-breaking terms on an equal footing. Variational minimization determines the optimal skyrmion texture, including both its spatial radial profile and internal $SU(4)$ spinor structure. We establish the phase diagram of skyrmion excitations, identify thirteen distinct skyrmion phases under different external-field conditions, and determine their spin-valley textures, energies, and characteristic sizes. The robustness of the results against different radial ansätze demonstrates the reliability of our variational approach. Our work provides a systematic framework for studying skyrmion excitations in multicomponent quantum Hall ferromagnets and can be naturally extended to more general $SU(4)$ quantum Hall systems.

</details>


### [130] [Correlated Insulator Moiré Bolometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00488)
*L. Elesin,A. L. Shilov,M. Kravtsov,X. Zhou,M. Lukianov,A. Kuksov,S. Jana,I. Iorsh,R. Izmaylov,K. Shein,I. Gayduchenko,T. Taniguchi,K. Watanabe,K. S. Novoselov,G. N. Goltsman,A. Principi,D. A. Bandurin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发现魔角扭曲双层石墨烯在半填充moire能带时，即使弱的长波长光子束也能通过选择性地加热低热容的电子子系统来抑制关联能隙，从而产生光子驱动的绝缘体-金属转变，实现宽带低噪声光响应，电压响应度超过每纳瓦吸收功率毫伏级别，建立了一个对微弱长波长辐射超灵敏探测的平台。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，光照射绝缘体通常不会使其转变为金属，除非涉及强超快激发导致的瞬态结构转变。作者旨在寻找一个例外情况：在魔角扭曲双层石墨烯中，弱光子即可驱动绝缘体-金属转变，以及探索其作为超灵敏长波长辐射探测器的潜力。

Method: 利用魔角扭曲双层石墨烯在moire能带半填充状态下的特性，通过弱长波长光子（能量与平带宽度相当）选择性地加热低热容的电子子系统，抑制关联能隙，从而产生巨电阻变化和光子驱动的绝缘体-金属转变。该机制与超导热电子响应具有对偶性，辐射加热的电子抑制多体有序态，但反向使关联绝缘体熔化为金属。

Result: 实现了宽带低噪声光响应，电压响应度超过每纳瓦吸收功率毫伏级别。该机制对几特斯拉的磁场具有鲁棒性，并呈现尖锐的绝缘体-金属电阻对比。巨电阻变化并非由持续的光载流子布居所主导，而是源于多体关联能隙对弱电子加热的极端敏感性。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了关联平带系统作为微弱长波长辐射超灵敏探测的平台，展示了一种与超导热电子响应机制对偶的新型光探测原理，为红外和太赫兹波段的高灵敏度探测器提供了新的材料体系和物理机制基础。

Abstract: Light incident on an insulator is generally not expected to turn it into a metal without invoking intense ultrafast excitation that leads to transient structural transitions. Here we show that magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene tuned to half filling of the moiré band provides a notable exception to this expectation. We find that weak beam of long-wavelength photons, with energies comparable to the flat-band width, selectively heat the low-heat-capacity electronic subsystem, thereby suppressing the correlated gap. This produces a giant resistance change governed not by a persistent photocarrier population, but by the extreme sensitivity of a many-body correlated gap to weak electronic heating. The resulting photon-driven insulator-to-metal transition produces a broadband low-noise photoresponse with voltage responsivity exceeding millivolts per nW of absorbed power. The mechanism is dual to superconducting hot-electron response: radiation-heated electrons suppress a many-body order, but in reverse the correlated insulator melts into a metal, providing robustness to magnetic fields of several tesla and a sharp insulator-to-metal resistive contrast. Our results establish correlated flat-band systems as a platform for ultra-sensitive detection of faint long-wavelength radiation.

</details>


### [131] [Spin selectivity induced by non-collinear spins in Rashba wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00599)
